- **jianmiezhan-jizhong-bingli**: 当用户资源有限但面临多个机会/威胁，需要在多个方向间做取舍时激活。典型触发信号： "5个客户机会同时出现""要不要同时做ToB和ToC""资源不够分怎么办"。 不调用场景：资源充裕可以多线并进、已经确定主攻方向只需执行、单一目标不存在取舍。 与"矛盾分析法"的区别：本skill解决"知道瓶颈后如何集中资源彻底解决"，后者解决"找到瓶颈在哪"。 ([kangarooking/mao-selected-works-skill](https://github.com/kangarooking/mao-selected-works-skill/tree/HEAD/jianmiezhan-jizhong-bingli) · ★133 · 7 copies)
- **jianmiezhan-jizhong-bingli**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解在关键局部集中绝对优势兵力的聚焦突破法时。 关键信号："怎么集中兵力？"、"歼灭战集中兵力？"、"聚焦突破？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是星火燎原（用 xingxingzhihuo-genjudi），或用户需要的是十六字诀（用 shiliuzijue）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/mao-xuan/jianmiezhan-jizhong-bingli) · ★1)
- **jianming-zhongwen**: 用简明技术中文写作或改写：短句、实义动词、条件在前、情态词按阶梯、 一词一义、术语首次出现时定义、只说事实不说重要性。用于 README、 操作手册、故障排查、报错文本、发布说明、事故报告、接口说明，以及给 领域外读者的技术说明。用户说「简明中文」「简明技术中文」「写得简单点」 「去掉废话」「说人话」「改成技术文档风格」「检查这段中文」时也启用。 回复同样遵守：第一句给答案，最多 5 句，只用散文。 ([heichaowo/jianming-zhongwen](https://github.com/heichaowo/jianming-zhongwen/tree/HEAD/skills/jianming-zhongwen) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **jianmo-skill**: Lightweight expert guidance for mathematical modeling competitions (such as CUMCM, COMAP MCM/ICM, APMCM) and real-world mathematical modeling tasks. Use when translating a complex scenario into mathematical structures, selecting or evaluating model families, implementing numerical solutions, conducting claim-driven validation, or drafting publication-grade modeling papers. Provides core modeling principles, problem pattern recognition, evidence-based research practices, scientific visualization, paper argumentation, and competition-aware review. - **jiao-di-cycle**: 当用户身处「我弱敌强」的格局，面对一个正在连胜、连取、连扩而越来越骄的对手，想知道该正面硬顶、还是让步、还是等——以及怎么把对方的势转化为自己的机会时使用。 把《資治通鑑》周紀一第3段任章谏魏桓子一事收成一套可执行的循环机制：先判对手是否已进入「胜而骄、骄而轻敌、轻敌而树敌」的循环（伐功矜能／拒谏／树敌三症状）， 再把「姑予」当作有标的、有反收节点的设计动作（予地→敌骄轻敌→盟友相亲→择交而图），等窗口打开时一击，并反向自检我方有没有走同一条路。 适用：竞争策略（跟不跟价格战、要不要正面对撞）、谈判让步设计（让什么、换什么、何时收）、资源换时间的交换条件、连胜对手的评估与等待判据。 不做：预测吉凶、择日、给历史人物翻案或做道德褒贬、把史事当寓言朗诵、替代具体行业的一线判断。 不用于：伙伴／盟友链条正在被侵蚀、要判连锁传导与断链后果（用 chunchi-structure）；双方均势无人得势、或还没有对手的开局前校准（用 five-affairs-seven-questions）。 English: Use when facing a stronger, currently-winning rival: judge whether it has entered the arrogant-vulnerable loop, design a bounded concession ("g… ([yihexiang/rulai-distill](https://github.com/yihexiang/rulai-distill/tree/HEAD/books/tongjian-wei-bundle/skills/jiao-di-cycle) · ★2)

- **jiaoyan-writing**: 教研写作助手。当语文教师需要撰写课题申报书、教研论文、教学案例、教学反思、研究报告，或需要解析 PDF 文献、生成符合职称评审规范的 Word 排版时触发。内置 pdfplumber（PDF 解析）与 python-docx（Word 排版）、AI 失败模式检查与写作质量检查。This skill helps Chinese-language teachers write research proposals, papers, teaching cases, and reports with built-in PDF parsing, Word formatting, AI failure mode checklist, and writing quality check. Trigger on "课题申报""教研论文""教学案例""教学反思""研究报告""写论文""课题立项""职称评审""PDF 解析""Word 排版""参考文献格式""查重率""GB/T 7714"。 ([ichangting/taotao-skills](https://github.com/ichangting/taotao-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jiaoyan-writing) · ★22)
- **jiaoyifu-workplanning**: 运营方案与工作规划核心技能。覆盖所有运营体系下的方案输出场景。 核心原则：用数据把问题锁死，用四层路径把解法锁死。 五节主结构（所有场景通用）：核心结论 → 现状诊断 → 目标确定 → 方法路径 → 执行方案。 全场景内置「脚姨夫视角评估」两级审核：①每模块输出后自动六维审核（承诺可兑现/执行不自欺/受益方清晰/逻辑一致/数字可追/病因命中）；②全模块确认后整合完整版，触发整体终审，用户确认后收尾。问题在模块内解决，不等整体完成后返工。 使用场景：用户说"工作规划"、"运营方案"、"服务体系方案"、"4月规划"、"季度规划"、 "全年规划"、"目标规划方案"、"活动方案"、"汇报材料"、"xxx方案"等运营/规划/汇报类任务。 触发关键词：工作规划、运营方案、服务体系方案、业务规划、月度规划、季度规划、年度规划、 活动方案、活动策划、工作汇报、复盘报告、增长策略、目标拆解。 替代技能：operation-plan、monthly-goal-planning、annual-strategic-planning、work-planning。 ([Jiaoyifu1203/jiaoyifu-workplanning](https://github.com/Jiaoyifu1203/jiaoyifu-workplanning/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **jiaoyiyuan-tony**: 该技能体现了李法师Tony作为一名资深交易员直接且务实的交易哲学。它旨在帮助用户建立专业的交易思维和系统，超越单纯的技术分析。该技能强调资金管理、心理韧性、独立思考在波动市场（尤其是加密货币）中实现长期稳定盈利的关键作用。它挑战传统观念，提倡拥抱趋势交易中“反人性”的本质，并提供一套有纪律的风险控制和财富积累框架。当您在交易中面临困惑、希望建立一套稳健的交易策略，或寻求对财富创造的真实现实视角时，可以运用此技能。 ([Ibook000/ibook-skill](https://github.com/Ibook000/ibook-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jiaoyiyuan-tony) · ★7)
- **jiaoyou-ruanjian**: 交友软件：流程、要点与避坑。实操指南。 ([yemugua/agent-skills](https://github.com/yemugua/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/love/dating/jiaoyou-ruanjian))

- **jiaoyuan**: 当用户要用毛泽东（“教员”）的方法论分析现实问题，做战略判断、矛盾分析、调查研究、组织动员、统一战线、复盘整风，或希望以教员的语气讲授、评点、代笔时调用。覆盖：实事求是、没有调查没有发言权、主要矛盾与矛盾主要方面、实践—认识循环、持久战与纸老虎、集中优势打歼灭战、群众路线、敌友与利益相关者、民主集中制与干部路线、党委会工作方法、团结—批评—团结、愚公移山与波浪式发展；也用于解读《毛泽东选集》篇目、诗词与画传中的人格和文风。不用于：冒充历史人物、政治煽动、攻击具体个人、伪造语录、预测股市彩票、把历史斗争话语套到日常人际关系上。Triggers: 教员/毛主席/毛泽东/毛选/矛盾论/实践论/论持久战/反对本本主义/调查研究/主要矛盾/群众路线/统一战线/集中优势/纸老虎/愚公移山/党委会的工作方法/沁园春/教员体/用毛选的方法 ([xuzhike123/jiaoyuan-skill](https://github.com/xuzhike123/jiaoyuan-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **jiaoyuan**: 触发：教员、毛泽东、毛选、用毛选的角度、实事求是、矛盾分析、主要矛盾、调查研究、集中兵力、统筹兼顾、统一战线、纸老虎、复盘审视、面对复杂问题不知从何入手。 以辩证方法论分析问题，先约束事实纪律，再按需调度思想武器；明显不适用时不加载。 English: Trigger on "jiaoyuan", "Mao Zedong", "Selected Works of Mao Zedong", "from Mao's perspective", seeking truth from facts, contradiction analysis, principal contradiction, investigation first, concentrate forces, overall planning, united front, paper tiger, self-review, or any complex problem without a clear entry point. Applies dialectical methodology, constrains fact discipline first, then dispatches a thinking tool only when clearly useful. - **jiaxiao-tuifei**: 面向驾校学员与家属的退费维权技能。当用户说「驾校不退费怎么办」「报名了不想学了能退多少钱」「驾校报名费能退吗」「科目一过了退学扣多少」「驾校拖延不给安排练车想退钱」「驾校合同写概不退费有效吗」「退学申请书怎么写」「投诉驾校打哪个电话」时使用。技能会按未开训/已过科一/已上实操等阶段核算应退金额区间（保守/合理/争取），识别「概不退费」等无效格式条款与驾校违约情形，输出退费申请书、材料清单、12328/12315/12345 投诉要点与小额诉讼路径。仅提供规则与文书，不代理案件、不承诺结果。 ([yemugua/agent-skills](https://github.com/yemugua/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/life-service/jiaxiao-tuifei-tianbao))

([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-contribution-framing) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-data-analysis**: Use when running and reporting the empirical analysis for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — cross-national measurement equivalence, common-method-variance checks, the right multilevel/dynamic-panel estimator, endogeneity and dynamic-endogeneity identification, and robustness aligned to the JIBS methods-editorial canon. Executes and reports; it does not design the study (jibs-methods) or frame the contribution (jibs-contribution-framing). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript within the international-business conversation — joining a live IB debate, problematizing rather than gap-spotting, and situating the work against the JIBS canon and adjacent IB outlets. Frames the conversation; it does not state the final contribution claim (jibs-contribution-framing). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-methods**: Use when designing the empirical study for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — matching a cross-country and/or multilevel design to the question, planning for measurement equivalence, common-method variance, and endogeneity before data collection. Designs the study; it does not run the estimation (jibs-data-analysis) or build the mechanism (jibs-theory-development). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-methods) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-rebuttal**: Use when planning revisions and drafting the point-by-point response letter after a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) revise-and-resubmit — prioritizing by the area editor's letter, executing the cross-national measurement, CMV, and (dynamic-)endogeneity work reviewers demand, and citing the relevant methods editorials. Drafts the revision and response; it does not interpret the decision (jibs-review-process). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-review-process**: Use when setting expectations for, or interpreting, the Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) editorial process — the desk-screen, double-blind review, area-editor routing by IB subfield, the methods-editorial standards reviewers apply, and how to read a JIBS decision letter. Explains the process; the response letter itself is drafted with jibs-rebuttal. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — the Springer Nature portal, double-blind anonymization, JIBS Style Guide formatting and the inclusive word limit, the DART Data Accessibility Statement and Code of Ethics, and required files. Checks readiness to submit; it does not handle the post-decision response (jibs-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-tables-figures**: Use when finalizing the tables and figures for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — cross-country descriptives, correlation/result tables, measurement-invariance and CMV diagnostic exhibits, cross-level interaction plots, and country-coverage maps, all in JIBS Style Guide form and counted inside the word limit. Polishes exhibits; do not invoke until the model and contribution are settled. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-theory-development**: Use when building the theoretical mechanism and deriving hypotheses for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — specifying cross-level (country/culture → firm/individual) logic, boundary conditions, and an explicit contribution to international-business theory. Builds the argument; it does not test it (jibs-data-analysis) or pick the design (jibs-methods). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-theory-development) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-topic-selection**: Use when choosing or sharpening a research question for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — testing whether it is a genuine international-business phenomenon with country/culture as a level of analysis, and whether JIBS (vs. AMJ/SMJ/JWB) is the right venue. Locks the question; it does not build the mechanism (jibs-theory-development). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-workflow**: Use when deciding which jibs-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from topic selection through rebuttal for a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Business-Studies-Skills/skills/jibs-workflow) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jibs-writing-style**: Use when polishing the prose of a Journal of International Business Studies (JIBS) manuscript — a phenomenon-forward narrative, an explicit IB contribution, disciplined cross-cultural terminology, the inclusive word budget, and JIBS Style Guide conventions (11-pt Times New Roman, double-spaced, author-date). - **jie-literature-positioning**: Use to position a Journal of International Economics (JIE) manuscript against the international-trade or open-economy-macro frontier — staking the contribution against gravity/structural-trade or international-finance literatures without writing a standalone survey. Sharpens positioning; it does not draft the paper. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-niaobushi**: 如厕训练：流程、要点与避坑。实操指南。 ([yemugua/agent-skills](https://github.com/yemugua/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/family/toddler/jie-niaobushi))
- **jie-rebuttal**: Use when drafting the response letter for a revise-and-resubmit at the Journal of International Economics (JIE) — answering international-trade or open-economy-macro referees on gravity/structural specification, identification, and model discipline, and keeping the revision within JIE's scope and originality bar. Structures the rebuttal; it does not invent results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-replication-and-data-policy**: Use when preparing the replication package for a Journal of International Economics (JIE) manuscript — JIE requires that all materials needed to replicate published papers (computer programs and data sets) be deposited in the journal's secure repository (Mendeley Data). Builds the deposit; it does not run your analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-replication-and-data-policy) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-review-process**: Use to understand how editorial handling and refereeing work at the Journal of International Economics (JIE) — the regular/short/PRP submission types, the Prior Review Process expedite option that carries decision letters and referee reports from AER/Econometrica/JPE/QJE/REStud, desk-reject odds, and (Co-)Editor routing between trade and international macro/finance. Sets expectations; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-shizhuang-bi**: 用户在创作短剧/网文，需要"借势装 X 自取其辱"类桥段：反派/配角借用不属于自己的资本（别人的名号、财富、权力、关系）到处装 X，最终被真正的拥有者戳穿、自取其辱时调用。 用于生成"虚假资本崩塌"的讽刺型打脸爽点。不适用于：资本真实但被更强碾压（那是 c03）；主角主动借用资本的策略（那是 b04 虚张声势）。 触发信号：借势装 X、狐假虎威、假身份、冒名顶替、戳穿（fake flex exposed）。 ([icooldp/400-duanju-shuangdian](https://github.com/icooldp/400-duanju-shuangdian/tree/HEAD/c-dalian-fanzhuan/c04-jie-shizhuang-bi) · ★14)
- **jie-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for the Journal of International Economics (JIE) via Elsevier's Editorial Manager — the non-refundable submission fee, the regular/short/PRP Article Type choice, the 150-word abstract, 1-7 keywords, suggesting a fitting (Co-)Editor, and reference completeness. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-tables-figures**: Use when building or auditing tables and figures for a Journal of International Economics (JIE) manuscript — gravity/coefficient tables, event-study and pass-through impulse-response plots, structural counterfactual charts, and self-contained notes that an international-trade or open-economy-macro referee can read standalone. Shapes exhibits; it does not generate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jie-topic-selection**: Use when checking whether a paper fits the Journal of International Economics (JIE) scope and clears its originality gate — trade patterns and commercial policy, exchange rates, open-economy macro, international finance, sovereign debt, factor mobility, spatial economics, and whether the motivation or modelling structure is original. Tests fit; it does not pick your topic for you. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Economics-Skills/skills/jie-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)

- **jie-workflow**: Use when deciding which jie-* sub-skill a Journal of International Economics (JIE) manuscript needs next — given where it stands (idea, identification, data, framing, exhibits, polish, replication, review, submission, rebuttal), this router names the next skill in the trade / international macro-finance pipeline.
